\displaystyle\Biggl|\int_{a_{N}^{(0)}}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{a_{N}^{(0)}}g(a_{N}^{(0)},x_{2})\mathrm{d}x_{1}\mathrm{d}x_{2}-\int_{a_{N}^{(0)}}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{a_{N}^{(0)}}g(x_{1},x_{2})\mathrm{d}x_{1}\mathrm{d}x_{2}\Biggr|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{a_{N}^{(0)}}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{a_{N}^{(0)}}|g(a_{N}^{(0)},x_{2})-g(x_{1},x_{2})|\mathrm{d}x_{1}\mathrm{d}x_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{a_{N}^{(0)}}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{a_{N}^{(0)}}\Biggl|\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{x_{1}}g(a_{N}^{(0)}+u(x_{1}-a_{N}^{(0)}),x_{2})\mathrm{d}u\Biggr||x_{1}-a_{N}^{(0)}|\mathrm{d}x_{1}\mathrm{d}x_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta_{N}^{1/2}\int_{a_{N}^{(0)}}^{\infty}|f^{\prime}(x_{2}^{2})|\int_{0}^{a_{N}^{(0)}}|x_{1}-a_{N}^{(0)}|\mathrm{d}x_{1}\mathrm{d}x_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Delta_{N}^{3/2}\int_{a_{N}^{(0)}}^{\infty}|f^{\prime}(x_{2}^{2})|\mathrm{d}x_{2}

\displaystyle=O(R_{2,N}),

we have that

I_{1}=\int_{a_{N}^{(0)}}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{a_{N}^{(0)}}g(x_{1},x_{2})\mathrm{d}x_{1}\mathrm{d}x_{2}+O(R_{2,N})
,

I_{2}=O(\delta^{-1}R_{1,N})

and

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=\int_{a_{N}^{(0)}}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{a_{N}^{(0)}}g(x_{1},x_{2})\mathrm{d}x_{1}\mathrm{d}x_{2}+O(R_{2,N})+O(\delta^{-1}R_{1,N})

\displaystyle=\int_{D_{N}^{(1)}}g(x)\mathrm{d}x+O\bigl(\delta^{-1}(R_{1,N}\lor R_{2,N})\bigr).

Therefore, the desired result can be obtained.
∎

Let

(5.26)

f_{\alpha}(s)=\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-s}}{s^{1+\alpha}},\quad f_{\alpha,\tau}(s)=\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-s})^{2}}{s^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\tau s}

for
\alpha\in(0,1)
and
\tau\geq 0
.

f_{\alpha}\in\mathcal{F}_{2\alpha}

and
f_{\alpha,\tau}\in\mathcal{F}_{-2+2\alpha}
.
Moreover, it holds that

(5.27)

\displaystyle\Delta_{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}f_{\alpha}(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N})e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)=\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{4\pi\alpha\theta_{2}}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}+O(\delta^{-3}\Delta_{N}^{1-\alpha}),

\displaystyle\Delta_{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}f_{\alpha,\tau}(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N})e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

(5.28)

\displaystyle=\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{4\pi\alpha\theta_{2}}\bigl(-\tau^{\alpha}+2(1+\tau)^{\alpha}-(2+\tau)^{\alpha}\bigr)\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}+O(\delta^{-3}\Delta_{N}^{1-\alpha}).

Proof.

Since

e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)=(1-\cos(2\pi k_{1}y))(1-\cos(2\pi k_{2}z))\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}
,
it holds from Lemma
5.1
that
for
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\beta}
,

\displaystyle\Delta_{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}f(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N})e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}\Biggl(\Delta_{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}f(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N})-\Delta_{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}f(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N})(\cos(2\pi k_{1}y)+\cos(2\pi k_{2}z))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Delta_{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}f(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N})\cos(2\pi k_{1}y)\cos(2\pi k_{2}z)\Biggr)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}\Biggl(\frac{1}{4\pi\theta_{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}f(s)\mathrm{d}s-\int_{D_{N}^{(1)}\cup D_{N}^{(2)}}f(\theta_{2}\pi^{2}|x|^{2})\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{D_{N}^{(1)}}f(\theta_{2}\pi^{2}|x|^{2})\mathrm{d}x+\int_{D_{N}^{(2)}}f(\theta_{2}\pi^{2}|x|^{2})\mathrm{d}x+O\bigl(\delta^{-3}(R_{1,N}\lor R_{2,N}\lor\Delta_{N}^{1-\beta/2})\bigr)\Biggr)

(5.29)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\pi\theta_{2}}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}\int_{0}^{\infty}f(s)\mathrm{d}s+O\bigl(\delta^{-3}(R_{1,N}\lor R_{2,N}\lor\Delta_{N}^{1-\beta/2})\bigr).

Since

\displaystyle f^{\prime}_{\alpha}(s)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{s^{2+\alpha}}\bigl(-(1-\mathrm{e}^{-s})(s+1+\alpha)+s\bigr),

\displaystyle f^{\prime\prime}_{\alpha}(s)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{s^{3+\alpha}}\bigl\{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-s})\bigl(s^{2}+2(1+\alpha)s+(1+\alpha)(2+\alpha)\bigr)-s\bigl(s+2(1+\alpha)\bigr)\bigr\},

1-\mathrm{e}^{-s}\sim s
(
s\to 0
)
and
1-\mathrm{e}^{-s}\sim 1
(
s\to\infty
),
it follows that

(5.30)

f_{\alpha}(s^{2})\sim\frac{1}{s^{2\alpha}},\quad f^{\prime}_{\alpha}(s^{2})\sim\frac{1}{s^{2+2\alpha}},\quad f^{\prime\prime}_{\alpha}(s^{2})\sim\frac{1}{s^{4+2\alpha}}\ (s\to 0),

f_{\alpha}(s^{2})\sim\frac{1}{s^{2+2\alpha}},\quad f^{\prime}_{\alpha}(s^{2})\sim\frac{1}{s^{4+2\alpha}},\quad f^{\prime\prime}_{\alpha}(s^{2})\sim\frac{1}{s^{6+2\alpha}}\ (s\to\infty),

and therefore
f_{\alpha}\in\mathcal{F}_{2\alpha}
.
Moreover, we have from (
5.30
) that

\displaystyle R_{1,N}

\displaystyle=O\Biggl(\Delta_{N}\biggl(\int_{\Delta_{N}^{1/2}}^{1}s|f_{\alpha}^{\prime}(s^{2})|\mathrm{d}s\lor\int_{\Delta_{N}^{1/2}}^{1}s^{3}|f_{\alpha}^{\prime\prime}(s^{2})|\mathrm{d}s\biggr)\Biggr)=O\biggl(\Delta_{N}\int_{\Delta_{N}^{1/2}}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}s}{s^{1+2\alpha}}\biggr)=O(\Delta_{N}^{1-\alpha}),

\displaystyle R_{2,N}

\displaystyle=O\biggl(\Delta_{N}^{3/2}\int_{\Delta_{N}^{1/2}}^{1}|f_{\alpha}^{\prime}(s^{2})|\mathrm{d}s\biggr)=O\biggl(\Delta_{N}^{3/2}\int_{\Delta_{N}^{1/2}}^{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}s}{s^{2+2\alpha}}\biggr)=O(\Delta_{N}^{1-\alpha})

and
R_{1,N}\lor R_{2,N}\lor\Delta_{N}^{1-\alpha}=O(\Delta_{N}^{1-\alpha})
.

In the same way, noting that

\displaystyle f^{\prime}_{\alpha,\tau}(s)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{e}^{-\tau s}}{s^{2+\alpha}}\bigl\{-(1-\mathrm{e}^{-s})^{2}\bigl((2-\tau)s+1+\alpha\bigr)+2s(1-\mathrm{e}^{-s})\bigr\},

\displaystyle f^{\prime\prime}_{\alpha,\tau}(s)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{e}^{-\tau s}}{s^{3+\alpha}}\bigl\{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-s})^{2}\bigl((\tau+2)^{2}s^{2}+2(1+\alpha)(\tau+2)s+(1+\alpha)(2+\alpha)\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad-4(1-\mathrm{e}^{-s})s(2s+1+\alpha+\tau)+2s^{2}\bigr\},

f_{\alpha,\tau}(s^{2})\sim s^{2-2\alpha},\quad f^{\prime}_{\alpha,\tau}(s^{2})\sim\frac{1}{s^{2\alpha}},\quad f^{\prime\prime}_{\alpha,\tau}(s^{2})\sim\frac{\tau\mathrm{e}^{-\tau s^{2}}}{s^{2+2\alpha}}\ (s\to 0),

f_{\alpha,\tau}(s^{2})\sim\frac{1}{s^{2+2\alpha}},\quad f^{\prime}_{\alpha,\tau}(s^{2})\sim\frac{\tau\mathrm{e}^{-\tau s^{2}}}{s^{2+2\alpha}}\leq\frac{1}{s^{4+2\alpha}},\quad f^{\prime\prime}_{\alpha,\tau}(s^{2})\sim\frac{\tau^{2}\mathrm{e}^{-\tau s^{2}}}{s^{2+2\alpha}}\leq\frac{1}{s^{6+2\alpha}}\ (s\to\infty),

we obtain that

f_{\alpha,\tau}\in\mathcal{F}_{-2+2\alpha}
and

R_{1,N}\lor R_{2,N}\lor\Delta_{N}^{1-(-1+\alpha)}=O(\Delta_{N}^{1-\alpha})
.

Since for
\beta>0
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\beta s}}{s^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{d}s=\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\infty}s^{(1-\alpha)-1}\mathrm{e}^{-\beta s}\mathrm{d}s=\frac{\beta^{\alpha}}{\alpha}\Gamma(1-\alpha),

it follows that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}f_{\alpha}(s)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{\alpha},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}f_{\alpha,\tau}(s)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\tau s}}{s^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{d}s+2\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-(1+\tau)s}}{s^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{d}s-\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-(2+\tau)s}}{s^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{\alpha}\bigl(-\tau^{\alpha}+2(1+\tau)^{\alpha}-(2+\tau)^{\alpha}\bigr).

Therefore, we obtain from (
5.29
)
that (
5.27
) and (
5.28
).
∎

Since
\Delta_{N}^{(\beta)}=o(1)
for
\beta<2

and (1) in Lemma
5.1
and Lemma
5.2
,
it follows that

(5.31)

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{N}^{1+\alpha}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}f_{\alpha}(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N})=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}),

(5.32)

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{N}^{1+\alpha}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}f_{\alpha,0}(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N})=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}).

For the coordinate process
x_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q}(t)
, let

\Delta_{i}x_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q}=x_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q}(t_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q}(t_{i-1})
.
By setting

\displaystyle A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}

\displaystyle=-\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}},

\displaystyle B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\sigma(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{\alpha/2}}\int_{0}^{(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}((i-1)\Delta_{N}-s)}\mathrm{d}w_{\boldsymbol{k}}(s),

\displaystyle B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{\alpha/2}}\int_{(i-1)\Delta_{N}}^{i\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i\Delta_{N}-s)}\mathrm{d}w_{\boldsymbol{k}}(s),

and
B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}=B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}+B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}
,
the increment
\Delta_{i}x_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}
can be expressed as

\Delta_{i}x_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}=A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}+B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}
.

Under Assumption
1
,
it holds that uniformly in
(y,z)\in D_{\delta}
,

(5.33)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}),

(5.34)

\displaystyle\sup_{j\geq 1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha/2}),

(5.35)

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)\cdots e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}(y,z)\Bigr|=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}).

Proof.

First of all, we show (
5.33
)-(
5.35
) under
(i), (iii) and (iv) in Assumption
1
.
It follows from (i) and (iv) that

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\Delta_{N}})\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}\bigl|\mathbb{E}[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\rangle_{\theta}]\bigr|

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{gk}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathbb{E}[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}.

Therefore, we have from (
5.31
) and (
5.32
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}),

and

\displaystyle\sup_{j\geq 1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{j\geq 1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}^{1+\alpha}}=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}).

Moreover, since (i), (iii), (iv) and

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)\cdots e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})\cdots(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}\Delta_{N}})\mathrm{e}^{-(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}+\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}})(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}+\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}})(j-1)\Delta_{N}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\bigl|\mathbb{E}[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}\cdots\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}\rangle_{\theta}]\bigr|

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{4}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathbb{E}[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{4}]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}\neq\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\bigl(\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}+2\mathrm{e}^{-(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}+\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}})(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}+\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}})(j-1)\Delta_{N}}\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\mathbb{E}[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{4}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\Biggr)\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i+j-2)\Delta_{N}}\Biggr)^{2},

it holds from (
5.31
) and (
5.32
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)\cdots e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{4}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\Biggr)\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\Biggr)\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}+\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\Biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}\lor\Delta_{N}^{2\alpha})

\displaystyle=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}).

We next prove (
5.33
)-(
5.35
)
under (ii)-(iv) in Assumption
1
.
We obtain from (ii), (iii) and the Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\Delta_{N}})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathbb{E}\bigl[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\rangle_{\theta}|\bigr]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Biggl[\biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|\biggr)^{2}\Biggr]

\displaystyle\leq\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}\mathbb{E}[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}]\Biggr)\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}

\displaystyle=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha})

and

\displaystyle\sup_{j\geq 1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{j\geq 1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\Delta_{N}})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(j-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathbb{E}\bigl[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\rangle_{\theta}|\bigr]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\Delta_{N}})\mathbb{E}\bigl[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\rangle_{\theta}|\bigr]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Biggl[\biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|\biggr)\biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|\biggr)\Biggr]

\displaystyle\leq\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\Biggr)^{1/2}\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\Biggr)^{1/2}\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}\mathbb{E}[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}]\Biggr)

\displaystyle=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha/2}).

Noting that for
\ell=3,4
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigl[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|^{\ell}\bigr]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigl[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{4}\bigr]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}\bigl[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2(\ell-2)}\bigr]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}),

\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})\mathbb{E}\bigl[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}\bigr]\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}),

and that for
j=0,1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{j}\mathbb{E}\bigl[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|\bigr]

\displaystyle\leq\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2j}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\Biggr)^{1/2}\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}\mathbb{E}\bigl[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}\bigr]\Biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}O(1),&j=0,\\
O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha/2}),&j=1,\end{cases}

we obtain from (ii)-(iv) that

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)\cdots e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})\cdots(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}\Delta_{N}})\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}\cdots\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}\rangle_{\theta}|]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathrm{e}^{-(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}+\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}})(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathrm{e}^{-(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}+\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}})(j-1)\Delta_{N}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})\cdots(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}\Delta_{N}})}{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}+\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}})\Delta_{N}})(1-\mathrm{e}^{-(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}+\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}})\Delta_{N}})}\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}\cdots\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}\rangle_{\theta}|]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}\mathbb{E}[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{4}]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigl[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}|^{3}\bigr]\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\rangle_{\theta}|]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\Delta_{N}})\mathbb{E}\bigl[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}\bigr]\mathbb{E}\bigl[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}\bigr]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\Delta_{N}})\mathbb{E}\bigl[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}\bigr]\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\rangle_{\theta}|]\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}\rangle_{\theta}|]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\Delta_{N}})(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}\Delta_{N}})\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}\rangle_{\theta}|]\cdots\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}\rangle_{\theta}|]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}\mathbb{E}[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{4}]

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigl[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|^{3}\bigr]\Biggr)\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|]\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})\mathbb{E}\bigl[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}\bigr]\Biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})\mathbb{E}\bigl[\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}^{2}\bigr]\Biggr)\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|]\Biggr)\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|]\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|]\Biggr)^{2}\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[|\langle\xi,e_{\boldsymbol{k}}\rangle_{\theta}|]\Biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}\lor\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}\lor\Delta_{N}^{2\alpha}\lor\Delta_{N}^{3\alpha/2}\lor\Delta_{N}^{\alpha})

\displaystyle=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}).

∎

It holds that uniformly in
(y,z)\in D_{\delta}
,

(5.36)

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z)=\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}\frac{\sigma^{2}\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{4\pi\alpha\theta_{2}}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}+r_{N,i}+O(\Delta_{N}),

where
\sum_{i=1}^{N}|r_{N,i}|=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha})
.
Moreover, it holds that uniformly in
(y,z)\in D_{\delta}
,

(5.37)

\displaystyle\sup_{i\neq j}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z)\Bigr|=O(1).

Proof.

First of all, we prove (
5.36
).
Noting that

\mathbb{E}[B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]=0
,

\mathbb{E}[B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]=\mathbb{E}[B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]=0

(
\boldsymbol{k}\neq\boldsymbol{\ell}
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}})^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{\alpha}}\int_{0}^{(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}((i-1)\Delta_{N}-s)}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}})^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{\alpha}}\int_{(i-1)\Delta_{N}}^{i\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i\Delta_{N}-s)}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})(1+\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})}{2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}},

we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\bigl(\mathbb{E}[(B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}})^{2}]+\mathbb{E}[(B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}})^{2}]\bigr)e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\bigl((1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})(1-\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}})+(1+\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})\bigr)e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\biggl(1-\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{2}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\biggr)e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

(5.38)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)+s_{N,i},

where

\displaystyle s_{N,i}=-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z).

It follows from Lemma
5.2
on fixed
\delta>0

that uniformly in
(y,z)\in D_{\delta}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{N}^{1+\alpha}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}f_{\alpha}(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N})e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

(5.39)

\displaystyle=\frac{\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{4\pi\alpha\theta_{2}}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}+O(\Delta_{N}),

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i-1)\Delta_{N}}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{N}^{1+\alpha}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}f_{\alpha,2(i-1)}(\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N})e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{4\pi\alpha\theta_{2}}J(i)\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}+O(\Delta_{N})

(5.40)

\displaystyle=:r_{N,i}+O(\Delta_{N}),

where

J(i)=-(2(i-1))^{\alpha}+2(1+2(i-1))^{\alpha}-(2+2(i-1))^{\alpha}
.
Noting that for
|x|<1
,

\displaystyle(1+x)^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m\geq 0}{\alpha\choose m}x^{m},\ \text{where }{\alpha\choose m}=\frac{\alpha(\alpha-1)\cdots(\alpha-m+1)}{m!},

and that

\displaystyle\biggl|{\alpha\choose m}\biggr|

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha(1-\alpha)\cdots(m-1-\alpha)}{m!}\leq\frac{\alpha}{m}\leq 1,

we obtain that for
i\geq 3
,

\displaystyle J(i)

\displaystyle=\bigl(2(i-1)\bigr)^{\alpha}\Biggl\{2\biggl(1+\frac{1}{2(i-1)}\biggr)^{\alpha}-1-\biggl(1+\frac{1}{i-1}\biggr)^{\alpha}\Biggr\}

\displaystyle=\bigl(2(i-1)\bigr)^{\alpha}\Biggl\{2\Biggl(1+\frac{\alpha}{2(i-1)}+\sum_{m\geq 2}{\alpha\choose m}\biggl(\frac{1}{2(i-1)}\biggr)^{m}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-1-\Biggl(1+\frac{\alpha}{i-1}+\sum_{m\geq 2}{\alpha\choose m}\biggl(\frac{1}{i-1}\biggr)^{m}\Biggr)\Biggr\}

\displaystyle=\bigl(2(i-1)\bigr)^{\alpha}\sum_{m\geq 2}{\alpha\choose m}\biggl(\frac{1}{2^{m-1}}-1\biggr)\frac{1}{(i-1)^{m}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{(i-1)^{2-\alpha}},

that is,

\sum_{i\geq 1}J(i)<\infty
.
Therefore, we obtain that

\sum_{i=1}^{N}|r_{N,i}|\lesssim\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}\sum_{i=1}^{N}J(i)\lesssim\Delta_{N}^{\alpha},

and from (
5.38
)-(
5.40
), we have (
5.36
).

Next, we prove (
5.37
).
Without loss of generality, consider

\displaystyle\sup_{i<j}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z)\Bigr|.

Since for
i<j
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{1,j,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{\alpha}}\int_{0}^{(i-1)\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}((i+j-2)\Delta_{N}-2s)}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}(\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-i)\Delta_{N}}-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i+j-2)\Delta_{N}}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{2,j,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{2,j,k}^{Q_{1}}]=0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{1,j,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}]

\displaystyle=-\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{\alpha}}\int_{(i-1)\Delta_{N}}^{i\Delta_{N}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}((i+j-1)\Delta_{N}-2s)}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=-\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-i-1)\Delta_{N}}(1+\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}),

it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{1,j,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}]+\mathbb{E}[B_{1,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{2,j,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}]+\mathbb{E}[B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{1,j,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}]+\mathbb{E}[B_{2,i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{2,j,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}]\Bigr|e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\bigl|\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-i)\Delta_{N}}-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i+j-2)\Delta_{N}}-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-i-1)\Delta_{N}}(1+\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})\bigr|e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-i-1)\Delta_{N}}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)+\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i+j-2)\Delta_{N}}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

\displaystyle=:\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-i-1)\Delta_{N}}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)+s_{i,j}.

Noting that

\displaystyle\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-i-1)\Delta_{N}}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)=\frac{\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}{4\pi\theta_{2}}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}J(j-i)+O(\Delta_{N}),

\sup_{i\geq 1}\sum_{j-i\geq 1}J(j-i)\leq\sum_{k\geq 1}J(k)<\infty

and

\displaystyle(0\leq)\ \sup_{i\geq 1}\sum_{j=1}^{N}s_{i,j}

\displaystyle=\sup_{i\geq 1}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(i+j-2)\Delta_{N}}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}),

we see that

\displaystyle\sup_{i<j}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y_{1},z_{1})e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y_{2},z_{2})\Bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{i\geq 1}\sum_{j-i\geq 1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{2}(1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{N}})^{2}}{2\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}(j-i-1)\Delta_{N}}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{2}(y,z)+\sup_{i\geq 1}\sum_{j=1}^{N}s_{i,j}

\displaystyle=O(1).

∎

5.2.
Proofs of Proposition
3.1
, Theorems
3.2
and
3.3

Proof of Proposition
3.1
.

Since
\delta
is fixed, it holds from

\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]=0
, Lemmas
5.3
and
5.4
that
uniformly in
(y,z)\in D_{\delta}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y,z)]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{i}x_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}\Delta_{i}x_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{\ell}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z)+\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}\frac{\sigma^{2}\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{4\pi\alpha\theta_{2}}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\theta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta_{1}}{\theta_{2}}z}+r_{N,i}+O(\Delta_{N}),

where

\sum_{i=1}^{N}|r_{N,i}|=O(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha})
, and
this completes the proof.
∎

Proof of Theorem
3.2
.

Let

\nu=(s,\kappa,\eta)^{\mathsf{T}}
,
\nu^{*}=(s^{*},\kappa^{*},\eta^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}
,
and
\hat{\nu}=(\hat{s},\hat{\kappa},\hat{\eta})^{\mathsf{T}}
.
Set

g_{\nu}(y,z)=\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{4\pi\alpha}s\,\mathrm{e}^{-(\kappa y+\eta z)}
,

\xi_{N}(y,z,\nu)=Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(y,z)-g_{\nu}(y,z)
and
\xi_{N}^{*}(y,z)=\xi_{N}(y,z,\nu^{*})
.
Note that

\displaystyle\partial{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{2\pi\alpha}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\xi_{N}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}},\nu)\mathrm{e}^{-(\kappa\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}}+\eta\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})}(1,-s\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},-s\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}}),

(5.41)

\displaystyle\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\xi_{N}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}},\nu)K_{\nu}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}L_{\nu}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}}),

where

\displaystyle K_{\nu}(y,z)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{2\pi\alpha}\mathrm{e}^{-(\kappa y+\eta z)}\begin{pmatrix}0&-y&-z\\
-y&sy^{2}&syz\\
-z&syz&sz^{2}\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle L_{\nu}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)^{2}}{8(\pi\alpha)^{2}}\mathrm{e}^{-2(\kappa y+\eta z)}\begin{pmatrix}1&-sy&-sz\\
-sy&s^{2}y^{2}&s^{2}yz\\
-sz&s^{2}yz&s^{2}z^{2}\end{pmatrix}.

By the Taylor expansion,

\displaystyle-\partial{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}=\partial{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\hat{\nu})^{\mathsf{T}}-\partial{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}=\int_{0}^{1}\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*}+u(\hat{\nu}-\nu^{*}))\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\nu}-\nu^{*}),

that is, for
\gamma<\frac{1}{2}\land(1-\alpha)-\frac{\rho}{2}
,

\displaystyle-\frac{N^{\gamma}}{m^{1/2}}\partial{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}=\frac{1}{m}\int_{0}^{1}\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*}+u(\hat{\nu}-\nu^{*}))\mathrm{d}u\,m^{1/2}N^{\gamma}(\hat{\nu}-\nu^{*}).

Let

\displaystyle L(\nu)=\frac{1}{|D_{\delta}|}\iint_{D_{\delta}}L_{\nu}(y,z)\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z.

Note that by computing the determinant of
L(\nu)
and the Schwartz inequality,

\displaystyle\det L(\nu)

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)^{2}}{8(\pi\alpha)^{2}|D_{\delta}|}\biggr)^{3}\int_{\delta}^{1-\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-2\kappa y}\mathrm{d}y\int_{\delta}^{1-\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-2\eta z}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Biggl(\int_{\delta}^{1-\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-2\kappa y}\mathrm{d}y\int_{\delta}^{1-\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-2\kappa y}s^{2}y^{2}\mathrm{d}y-\biggl(\int_{\delta}^{1-\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-2\kappa y}sy\mathrm{d}y\biggr)^{2}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Biggl(\int_{\delta}^{1-\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-2\eta z}\mathrm{d}z\int_{\delta}^{1-\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-2\eta z}s^{2}z^{2}\mathrm{d}z-\biggl(\int_{\delta}^{1-\delta}\mathrm{e}^{-2\eta z}sz\mathrm{d}z\biggr)^{2}\Biggr)

\displaystyle>0,

and
L(\nu)
is non-singular.
To complete the proof, it suffice to show that

(5.42)

\hat{\nu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\nu^{*},

(5.43)

\frac{\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*})}{m}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}L(\nu^{*}),

(5.44)

\frac{1}{m}\sup_{|\nu-\nu^{*}|\leq\epsilon_{N}}|\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu)-\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*})|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0\quad\text{for }\epsilon_{N}\downarrow 0,

(5.45)

-\frac{N^{\gamma}}{m^{1/2}}\partial{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

First, note that

(5.46)

\displaystyle\sup_{(y,z)\in D_{\delta}}\mathbb{E}[Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(y,z)]

\displaystyle\lesssim 1,

(5.47)

\displaystyle\sup_{(y,z)\in D_{\delta}}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(y,z)^{2}]

\displaystyle=O(\Delta_{N}^{1\land 2(1-\alpha)}).

We can see from Proposition
3.1
that (
5.46
) holds.

Proof of (
5.47
).

It follows that

\displaystyle\sup_{(y,z)\in D_{\delta}}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(y,z)^{2}]\leq\sup_{(y,z)\in D_{\delta}}\mathrm{Var}[Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(y,z)]+\sup_{(y,z)\in D_{\delta}}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(y,z)]^{2}

and from Proposition
3.1
, one has

\sup_{(y,z)\in D_{\delta}}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(y,z)]^{2}=O(\Delta_{N}^{2(1-\alpha)})
.
It also follows from the Isserlis’ theorem that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}[Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(y,z)]=\mathbb{E}[Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(y,z)^{2}]-\mathbb{E}[Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(y,z)]^{2}

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\Bigl(\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y,z)(\Delta_{j}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y,z)]-\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y,z)]\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{j}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y,z)]\Bigr)

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl(\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}]+2\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]\mathbb{E}[B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}^{Q_{1}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+4\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}^{Q_{1}}]+\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}^{Q_{1}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}^{Q_{1}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]\mathbb{E}[A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}]-2\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}^{Q_{1}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}^{Q_{1}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}^{Q_{1}}]\mathbb{E}[B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}^{Q_{1}}]\Bigr)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)\cdots e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}(y,z)

\displaystyle\lesssim\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl(\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}]+\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]\mathbb{E}[A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]\mathbb{E}[B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}^{Q_{1}}]\Bigr)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)\cdots e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}(y,z)

\displaystyle\qquad+\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}^{Q_{1}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}^{Q_{1}}]\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}^{Q_{1}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)\cdots e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}(y,z)

\displaystyle\qquad+\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}\sum_{i\neq j}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl(\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}^{Q_{1}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}^{Q_{1}}]\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}^{Q_{1}}]\Bigr)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)\cdots e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}(y,z)

\displaystyle=:S_{1}+S_{2}+S_{3}.

From Lemmas
5.3
and
5.4
, we have

\displaystyle S_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}\Biggl\{\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{3}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)\cdots e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{4}}(y,z)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|\Biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Biggl(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Biggl(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}^{Q_{1}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|\Biggr)\Biggr\}

\displaystyle=O\Biggl(\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}\lor\Delta_{N}^{2\alpha}\lor\Delta_{N}^{2\alpha-1})\Biggr)=O(\Delta_{N}),

\displaystyle S_{2}

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Biggl(\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}^{Q_{1}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle=O\Biggl(\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}\Delta_{N}^{2\alpha-1}\Biggr)=O(\Delta_{N}),

\displaystyle S_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}\Biggl\{\Biggl(\sup_{j\geq 1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}A_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\Biggl(\sup_{i\neq j}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Biggl(\sup_{j\neq i}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1},\boldsymbol{k}_{2}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}^{Q_{1}}B_{j,\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}^{Q_{1}}]e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{1}}(y,z)e_{\boldsymbol{k}_{2}}(y,z)\Bigr|\Biggr)^{2}\Biggr\}

\displaystyle=O\Biggl(\biggl(\frac{1}{N\Delta_{N}^{\alpha}}\biggr)^{2}(\Delta_{N}^{\alpha/2}\lor 1)\Biggr)=O(\Delta_{N}^{2(1-\alpha)}),

and
\sup_{(y,z)\in D_{\delta}}\mathrm{Var}[Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(y,z)]=O(\Delta_{N}^{1\land 2(1-\alpha)})
.
Therefore, we obtain (
5.47
).

Note also that
for any continuous function
h(y,z)
with respect to
(y,z)\in D_{\delta}
,

(5.48)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}h(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})\to\frac{1}{|D_{\delta}|}\iint_{D_{\delta}}h(y,z)\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z,

and that for any continuous function
h_{\nu}(y,z)
with respect to

(y,z,\nu)\in D_{\delta}\times\Xi_{1}
,

(5.49)

\displaystyle\sup_{\nu\in\Xi_{1}}\Biggl|\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}h_{\nu}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})-\frac{1}{|D_{\delta}|}\iint_{D_{\delta}}h_{\nu}(y,z)\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z\Biggr|\to 0.

We will show (
5.42
)-(
5.45
).

Proof of (
5.42
).

Let

\displaystyle U(\nu,\nu^{*})=\frac{1}{|D_{\delta}|}\iint_{D_{\delta}}(g_{\nu}(y,z)-g_{\nu^{*}}(y,z))^{2}\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z.

For the proof of the consistency, it is enough to show that

(i)

If
U(\nu,\nu^{*})=0
, then
\nu=\nu^{*}
,

(ii)

\displaystyle\sup_{\nu\in\Xi_{1}}\biggl|\frac{U_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu)}{m}-U(\nu,\nu^{*})\biggr|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0
.

If
U(\nu,\nu^{*})=0
, then
g_{\nu}(y,z)=g_{\nu^{*}}(y,z)
for any
(y,z)\in D_{\delta}
.
It implies that

\mathrm{e}^{-(\kappa y+\eta z)}s=\mathrm{e}^{-(\kappa^{*}y+\eta^{*}z)}s^{*}

for any
(y,z)\in D_{\delta}
, that is,

\nu=\nu^{*}
.

Let
h_{\nu}(y,z)=g_{\nu^{*}}(y,z)-g_{\nu}(y,z)
.
It follows from
\xi_{N}(y,z,\nu)=\xi_{N}^{*}(y,z)+h_{\nu}(y,z)
that

\displaystyle\sup_{\nu\in\Xi_{1}}\biggl|\frac{U_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu)}{m}-U(\nu,\nu^{*})\biggr|

\displaystyle=\sup_{\nu\in\Xi_{1}}\Biggl|\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\xi_{N}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}},\nu)^{2}-\frac{1}{|D_{\delta}|}\iint_{D_{\delta}}h_{\nu}(y,z)^{2}\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z\Biggr|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})^{2}+2\sup_{(y,z,\nu)\in D_{\delta}\times\Xi_{1}}|h_{\nu}(y,z)|\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}|\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})|

(5.50)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{\nu\in\Xi_{1}}\Biggl|\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}h_{\nu}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})^{2}-\frac{1}{|D_{\delta}|}\iint_{D_{\delta}}h_{\nu}(y,z)^{2}\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z\Biggr|.

According to (
5.47
), (
5.49
) and the Schwartz inequality,
it holds that

(5.51)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})^{2}]=o(1),\quad\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\mathbb{E}[|\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})|]=o(1)

and

(5.52)

\displaystyle\sup_{\nu\in\Xi_{1}}\Biggl|\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}h_{\nu}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})^{2}-\frac{1}{|D_{\delta}|}\iint_{D_{\delta}}h_{\nu}(y,z)^{2}\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z\Biggr|\to 0.

Therefore,
we obtain from (
5.50
)-(
5.52
) and
the boundedness of
h_{\nu}(y,z)
that

\displaystyle\sup_{\nu\in\Xi_{1}}\biggl|\frac{U_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu)}{m}-U(\nu,\nu^{*})\biggr|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

Proof of (
5.43
).

It follows
from (
5.47
), (
5.48
) and
the boundedness of
K_{\nu}(y,z)
on
D_{\delta}\times\Xi_{1}
that

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\mathbb{E}\bigl[\bigl|\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})K_{\nu}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})\bigr|\bigr]=o(1)

and that

\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}L_{\nu^{*}}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})\to\frac{1}{|D_{\delta}|}\iint_{D_{\delta}}L_{\nu^{*}}(y,z)\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z=L(\nu^{*}).

Therefore, we see from (
5.41
) that (
5.43
) holds.

Proof of (
5.44
).

From (
5.41
),
there exist uniformly continuous functions

V_{1}(y,z,\nu)
and
V_{2}(y,z,\nu)

with respect to
(y,z,\nu)\in D_{\delta}\times\Xi_{1}
such that

\displaystyle\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu)=\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})V_{1}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}},\nu)+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}V_{2}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}},\nu).

Let
\epsilon>0
and
\epsilon_{N}\downarrow 0
.
It follows from the uniform continuity of
V_{k}
on
D_{\delta}\times\Xi_{1}
that

\displaystyle\sup_{(y,z)\in D_{\delta}}\sup_{|\nu-\nu^{*}|\leq\epsilon_{N}}|V_{k}(y,z,\nu)-V_{k}(y,z,\nu^{*})|\leq\epsilon

for large
N
, and that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sup_{|\nu-\nu^{*}|\leq\epsilon_{N}}|\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu)-\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*})|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sup_{|\nu-\nu^{*}|\leq\epsilon_{N}}\biggl|\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\Bigl(Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})\{V_{1}(y_{j_{2}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}},\nu)-V_{1}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}},\nu^{*})\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\{V_{2}(y_{j_{2}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}},\nu)-V_{2}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}},\nu^{*})\}\Bigr)\biggr|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\Bigl(Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})\sup_{(y,z)\in D_{\delta}}\sup_{|\nu-\nu^{*}|\leq\epsilon_{N}}|V_{1}(y,z,\nu)-V_{1}(y,z,\nu^{*})|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sup_{(y,z)\in D_{\delta}}\sup_{|\nu-\nu^{*}|\leq\epsilon_{N}}|V_{2}(y,z,\nu)-V_{2}(y,z,\nu^{*})|\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\epsilon}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}(Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})+1).

Therefore, we obtain from (
5.46
) that for large
N
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\biggl[\frac{1}{m}\sup_{|\nu-\nu^{*}|\leq\epsilon_{N}}|\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu)-\partial^{2}{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*})|\biggr]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\epsilon}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\mathbb{E}[Z_{N}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})+1]\lesssim\epsilon,

and the proof of (
5.44
) is complete.

Proof of (
5.45
).

Let
\varphi_{\nu}(y,z)=\mathrm{e}^{-(\kappa y+\eta z)}(1,-sy,-sz)^{\mathsf{T}}
.
Noting that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(y,z)]=O(\Delta_{N}^{1-\alpha}),\quad\mathbb{E}\bigl[(\xi_{N}^{*}(y,z)-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(y,z)])^{2}\bigr]=O(\Delta_{N}^{1\land 2(1-\alpha)})

uniformly in
(y,z)\in D_{\delta}
,
we obtain from
m=O(N^{\rho})
and

\frac{\rho}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\land(1-\alpha)+\gamma<0
that

\displaystyle-\frac{N^{\gamma}}{m^{1/2}}\partial{U}_{N,m}^{(1)}(\nu^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}

\displaystyle=\frac{N^{\gamma}}{m^{1/2}}\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{2\pi\alpha}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\bigl(\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})]\bigr)\varphi_{\nu^{*}}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{N^{\gamma}}{m^{1/2}}\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{2\pi\alpha}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})]\varphi_{\nu^{*}}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})

\displaystyle\leq m^{1/2}N^{\gamma}\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{2\pi\alpha}\Biggl(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\bigl(\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})]\bigr)^{2}\Biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Biggl(\frac{1}{m}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}|\varphi_{\nu^{*}}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})|^{2}\Biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{N^{\gamma}}{m^{1/2}}\frac{\Gamma(1-\alpha)}{2\pi\alpha}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{m_{2}}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{N}^{*}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})]\varphi_{\nu^{*}}(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}},\widetilde{z}_{j_{2}})

\displaystyle=O_{p}\bigl(m^{1/2}N^{-\frac{1}{2}\land(1-\alpha)+\gamma}\bigr)+O\bigl(m^{1/2}N^{-(1-\alpha)+\gamma}\bigr)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1).

∎

Proof of Theorem
3.3
.

(2) For any
\boldsymbol{r}=(r_{1},r_{2})
,
r_{1}.r_{2}\geq 1
, let

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{n}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl\{(\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i}))-(\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))\bigr\}^{2}.

Note that

\displaystyle\Biggl|\hat{\sigma}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))^{2}\Biggr|

\displaystyle=\Biggl|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))^{2}\Biggr|

\displaystyle=\Biggl|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl\{(\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i}))-(\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))\bigr\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl\{(\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i}))-(\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))\bigr\}(x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))\Biggr|

(5.53)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{A}_{n}+\mathcal{A}_{n}^{1/2}\Biggl(\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))^{2}\Biggr)^{1/2}

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))^{2}=O_{p}(1).

Let
\Delta_{n}=\lfloor\frac{N}{n}\rfloor\frac{1}{N}
,

D_{j_{1},j_{2}}=(y_{j_{1}-1},y_{j_{1}}]\times(z_{j_{2}-1},z_{j_{2}}]
,

\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y,z)=X_{\widetilde{t}_{i}}^{Q_{1}}(y,z)-X_{\widetilde{t}_{i-1}}^{Q_{1}}(y,z)

and

\Delta_{i}x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}=x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1})
.
Since

\displaystyle\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1})

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{M}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})\sin(\pi r_{1}y_{j_{1}})\sin(\pi r_{2}z_{j_{2}})\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\hat{\kappa}}{2}y_{j_{1}}}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\hat{\eta}}{2}z_{j_{2}}},

\displaystyle x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1})

\displaystyle=2\iint_{D}\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y,z)\sin(\pi r_{1}y)\sin(\pi r_{2}z)\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\kappa^{*}}{2}y}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\eta^{*}}{2}z}\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z,

it follows that

\displaystyle(\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i}))-(\hat{x}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{M}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})\sin(\pi r_{1}y_{j_{1}})\sin(\pi r_{2}z_{j_{2}})\bigl(\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\hat{\kappa}}{2}y_{j_{1}}}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\hat{\eta}}{2}z_{j_{2}}}-\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\kappa^{*}}{2}y_{j_{1}}}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\eta^{*}}{2}z_{j_{2}}}\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}\iint_{D_{j_{1},j_{2}}}\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\bigl(\sin(\pi r_{1}y_{j_{1}})\sin(\pi r_{2}z_{j_{2}})\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\kappa^{*}}{2}y_{j_{1}}}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\eta^{*}}{2}z_{j_{2}}}-\sin(\pi r_{1}y)\sin(\pi r_{2}z)\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\kappa^{*}}{2}y}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\eta^{*}}{2}z}\bigr)\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}\iint_{D_{j_{1},j_{2}}}\bigl(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y,z)\bigr)\sin(\pi r_{1}y)\sin(\pi r_{2}z)\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\kappa^{*}}{2}y}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\eta^{*}}{2}z}\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=:2(\mathcal{B}_{1,i}+\mathcal{B}_{2,i}+\mathcal{B}_{3,i})

and that

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{n}\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\mathcal{B}_{1,i}^{2}+\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{2}+\mathcal{B}_{3,i}^{2}).

For the evaluation of
\mathcal{B}_{1,i}
,
we obtain from the Taylor expansion that

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{1,i}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})\sin^{2}(\pi r_{1}y_{j_{1}})\sin^{2}(\pi r_{2}z_{j_{2}})\bigl(\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\hat{\kappa}}{2}y_{j_{1}}}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\hat{\eta}}{2}z_{j_{2}}}-\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\kappa}{2}y_{j_{1}}}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\eta}{2}z_{j_{2}}}\bigr)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})\sin^{2}(\pi r_{1}y_{j_{1}})\sin^{2}(\pi r_{2}z_{j_{2}})

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Biggl\{\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})\exp\biggl(\frac{y_{j_{1}}}{2}(\kappa^{*}+u(\hat{\kappa}-\kappa^{*}))+\frac{z_{j_{2}}}{2}(\eta^{*}+u(\hat{\eta}-\eta^{*}))\biggr)\mathrm{d}u\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\kappa}-\kappa^{*}\\
\hat{\eta}-\eta^{*}\end{pmatrix}\Biggr\}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{mN^{2\gamma}}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Biggl|\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})\exp\biggl(\frac{y_{j_{1}}}{2}(\kappa^{*}+u(\hat{\kappa}-\kappa^{*}))+\frac{z_{j_{2}}}{2}(\eta^{*}+u(\hat{\eta}-\eta^{*}))\biggr)\mathrm{d}u\Biggr|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\times mN^{2\gamma}(|\hat{\kappa}-\kappa^{*}|^{2}+|\hat{\eta}-\eta^{*}|^{2})

\displaystyle=:\mathcal{C}_{1,i}\times mN^{2\gamma}(|\hat{\kappa}-\kappa^{*}|^{2}+|\hat{\eta}-\eta^{*}|^{2}).

Note that uniformly in
(y,z)\in D
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y,z)]\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{\alpha}}.

Let
\epsilon_{1}
,
\epsilon_{2}
be arbitrary positive numbers.
On
\Omega_{1}=\{|\hat{\kappa}-\kappa^{*}|+|\hat{\eta}-\eta^{*}|<\epsilon_{1}\}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{n}:=n\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{C}_{1,i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{n}{mN^{2\gamma}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}}),

and

\displaystyle P(|\mathcal{C}_{n}|>\epsilon_{2})

\displaystyle=P(\{|\mathcal{C}_{n}|>\epsilon_{2}\}\cap\Omega_{1})+P(\{|\mathcal{C}_{n}|>\epsilon_{2}\}\cap\Omega_{1}^{\mathrm{c}})

\displaystyle\leq P\Biggl(\frac{n}{mN^{2\gamma}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})\gtrsim\epsilon_{2}\Biggr)+P(\Omega_{1}^{\mathrm{c}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{n}{\epsilon_{2}mN^{2\gamma}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})]+P(\Omega_{1}^{\mathrm{c}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{n^{2-\alpha}}{\epsilon_{2}mN^{2\gamma}}+P(\Omega_{1}^{\mathrm{c}}).

From
\frac{n^{2-\alpha}}{mN^{2\gamma}}\to 0
and Theorem
3.2
,
one has
\mathcal{C}_{n}=o_{p}(1)
and

\displaystyle n\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{B}_{1,i}^{2}=o_{p}(1).

Noting that for the evaluation of
\mathcal{B}_{2,i}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}\iint_{D_{j_{1},j_{2}}}\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\bigl(\sin(\pi r_{1}y_{j_{1}})\sin(\pi r_{2}z_{j_{2}})\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\kappa}{2}y_{j_{1}}}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\eta}{2}z_{j_{2}}}-\sin(\pi r_{1}y)\sin(\pi r_{2}z)\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\kappa}{2}y}\mathrm{e}^{\frac{\eta}{2}z}\bigr)^{2}\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}})^{2}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})]\iint_{D_{j_{1},j_{2}}}\biggl(\frac{1}{M_{1}^{2}}+\frac{1}{M_{2}^{2}}\biggr)\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{\alpha}}\biggl(\frac{1}{M_{1}^{2}}+\frac{1}{M_{2}^{2}}\biggr)\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{\alpha}(M_{1}^{2}\land M_{2}^{2})},

one has that under

\frac{n^{2-\alpha+\epsilon}}{M_{1}^{2\epsilon}\land M_{2}^{2\epsilon}}\to 0
,

n\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{2}=O_{p}\biggl(\frac{n^{2-\alpha}}{M_{1}^{2}\land M_{2}^{2}}\biggr)=o_{p}(1).

For the evaluation of
\mathcal{B}_{3,i}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{3,i}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}\iint_{D_{j_{1},j_{2}}}\bigl(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y,z)\bigr)^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi r_{1}y)\sin^{2}(\pi r_{2}z)\mathrm{e}^{\kappa^{*}y}\mathrm{e}^{\eta^{*}z}\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{M_{1}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{M_{2}}\iint_{D_{j_{1},j_{2}}}\bigl(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y,z)\bigr)^{2}\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}z.

Since for
(y,z)\in D_{j_{1},j_{2}}
,

\partial_{y}e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)=2\Bigl(\pi k_{1}\cos(\pi k_{1}y)-\frac{\kappa}{2}\sin(\pi k_{1}y)\Bigr)\sin(\pi k_{2}z)\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\kappa}{2}y}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{\eta}{2}z},

|\partial e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)|\lesssim\Bigl|\pi k_{1}\cos(\pi k_{1}y)-\frac{\kappa}{2}\sin(\pi k_{1}y)\Bigr|+\Bigl|\pi k_{2}\cos(\pi k_{2}z)-\frac{\eta}{2}\sin(\pi k_{2}z)\Bigr|\lesssim|\boldsymbol{k}|,

\displaystyle\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y,z)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\Delta_{i}x_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}(e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)),

\displaystyle|e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|\partial e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y_{j_{1}}+u(y-y_{j_{1}}),z_{j_{2}}+u(z-z_{j_{2}}))\bigr|\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\qquad\times\bigl(|y-y_{j_{1}}|^{2}+|z-z_{j_{2}}|^{2}\bigr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\boldsymbol{k}|\biggl(\frac{1}{M_{1}^{2}}+\frac{1}{M_{2}^{2}}\biggr)^{1/2}\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1/2}}{M_{1}\land M_{2}}

and
|e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z)|\lesssim 1
, it holds that
for
\epsilon<\alpha
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigl[(\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-\Delta_{i}X^{Q_{1}}(y,z))^{2}\bigr]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{i}x_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}\Delta_{i}x_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}](e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z))(e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z))

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[A_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}A_{i,\boldsymbol{\ell}}](e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z))(e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z))

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\boldsymbol{k},\boldsymbol{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[B_{i,\boldsymbol{k}}^{Q_{1}}B_{i,\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{Q_{1}}](e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z))(e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{\ell}}(y,z))

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{n}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha}}(e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z))^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{n}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha-\epsilon}}\frac{(e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y_{j_{1}},z_{j_{2}})-e_{\boldsymbol{k}}(y,z))^{2}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{n}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha-\epsilon}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{\epsilon}}\biggl(\frac{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1/2}}{M_{1}\land M_{2}}\land 1\biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{n}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha-\epsilon}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{\epsilon}}\biggl(\frac{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1/2}}{M_{1}\land M_{2}}\biggr)^{2\epsilon}1^{2-2\epsilon}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{1}^{2\epsilon}\land M_{2}^{2\epsilon}}\sum_{\boldsymbol{k}\in\mathbb{N}^{2}}\frac{1-\mathrm{e}^{-\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}\Delta_{n}}}{\lambda_{\boldsymbol{k}}^{1+\alpha-\epsilon}}=O\biggl(\frac{1}{n^{\alpha-\epsilon}(M_{1}^{2\epsilon}\land M_{2}^{2\epsilon})}\biggr)

and that under

\frac{n^{2-\alpha+\epsilon}}{M_{1}^{2\epsilon}\land M_{2}^{2\epsilon}}\to 0
,

n\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{B}_{3,i}^{2}=O_{p}\biggl(\frac{n^{2-\alpha+\epsilon}}{M_{1}^{2\epsilon}\land M_{2}^{2\epsilon}}\biggr)=o_{p}(1).

Therefore,
it holds that
under
\frac{n^{2-\alpha}}{mN^{2\gamma}}\to 0
and

\frac{n^{2-\alpha+\epsilon}}{M_{1}^{2\epsilon}\land M_{2}^{2\epsilon}}\to 0
,

n\mathcal{A}_{n}=o_{p}(1)
and

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\Biggl(\hat{\sigma}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))^{2}\Biggr)=o_{p}(1).

Since

(5.54)

\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_{1,1}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{1,1}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))^{2}-(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2}\\
\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_{1,2}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i})-x_{1,2}^{Q_{1}}(\widetilde{t}_{i-1}))^{2}-(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\end{pmatrix}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\longrightarrow}}N\Biggl(0,\begin{pmatrix}2(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{4}&0\\
0&2(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{4}\end{pmatrix}\Biggr),

it follows that

(5.55)

\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2}\\
\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\end{pmatrix}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\longrightarrow}}N\Biggl(0,\begin{pmatrix}2(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{4}&0\\
0&2(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{4}\end{pmatrix}\Biggr).

Let

\displaystyle G(x,y)=\biggl(\frac{1}{y^{1/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{x^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}},\quad H(x,y)=(x^{-1}G(x,y))^{1/\alpha}.

Note that under

\frac{n^{2-\alpha}}{mN^{2\gamma}}\to 0
and

\frac{n^{2-\alpha+\epsilon}}{M_{1}^{2\epsilon}\land M_{2}^{2\epsilon}}\to 0
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\Biggl\{\biggl(\frac{1}{\hat{s}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}-\biggl(\frac{1}{s^{*}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\Biggr\}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{n}}{m^{1/2}N^{\gamma}}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{-1}{1-\alpha}\biggl(\frac{1}{s^{*}+u(\hat{s}-s^{*})}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}+1}\mathrm{d}u\,m^{1/2}N^{\gamma}(\hat{s}-s^{*})

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\Biggl[\biggl\{\frac{3\pi^{2}}{\hat{s}^{1/\alpha}}\biggl(\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-1}\biggr\}^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}-\biggl\{\frac{3\pi^{2}}{(s^{*})^{1/\alpha}}\biggl(\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-1}\biggr\}^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}\Biggl]

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\Biggl[\Biggl\{\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}}{\hat{s}^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}-\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}}{(s^{*})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}\Biggr\}\biggl(\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}}{(s^{*})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}\Biggl\{\biggl(\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}-\biggl(\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}\Biggr\}\Biggl]

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}}{(s^{*})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}\sqrt{n}\Biggl\{\biggl(\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}-\biggl(\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}\Biggr\}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}\theta_{2}^{*}}{(\sigma^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}\theta_{2}^{*}\sqrt{n}\Bigl(G(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2},\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2})-G\bigl((\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2},(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\bigr)\Bigl)+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}\theta_{2}^{*}}{(\sigma^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}\theta_{2}^{*}\partial G\bigl((\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2},(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\bigr)\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2}\\
\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(1)

and

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\hat{\lambda}_{1,1}-\lambda_{1,1}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\Biggl\{\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}}{\hat{s}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\biggl(\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}}{s^{*}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\biggl(\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\Biggr\}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\Biggl[\Biggl\{\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}}{\hat{s}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}-\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}}{s^{*}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\Biggr\}\biggl(\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}}{s^{*}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\Biggl\{\biggl(\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\biggl(\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\Biggr\}\Biggr]

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}}{s^{*}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\sqrt{n}\Biggl\{\biggl(\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\frac{1}{(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2})^{1/\alpha}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\biggl(\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}-\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{-\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\frac{1}{(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\Biggr\}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}\theta_{2}^{*}}{(\sigma^{*})^{2}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\sqrt{n}\Bigl(H(\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2},\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2})-H\bigl((\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2},(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\bigr)\Bigr)+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}\theta_{2}^{*}}{(\sigma^{*})^{2}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\partial H\bigl((\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2},(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\bigr)\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2}\\
\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(1).

It then follows from

\sigma^{2}=s\theta_{2}
,
\theta_{1}=\kappa\theta_{2}
and
\eta_{1}=\eta\theta_{2}
that

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\hat{\sigma}^{2}-(\sigma^{*})^{2})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}(\hat{s}\hat{\theta}_{2}-s^{*}\theta_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\bigl((\hat{s}-s^{*})\hat{\theta}_{2}+s^{*}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{*})\bigr)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{n}}{m^{1/2}N^{\gamma}}\cdot m^{1/2}N^{\gamma}(\hat{s}-s^{*})\hat{\theta}_{2}+s^{*}\sqrt{n}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle=s^{*}\sqrt{n}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{*})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}\theta_{2}^{*}}{(\sigma^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}(\sigma^{*})^{2}\partial G\bigl((\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2},(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\bigr)\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2}\\
\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{*})

\displaystyle=\kappa^{*}\sqrt{n}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{*})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}\theta_{2}^{*}}{(\sigma^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}\theta_{1}^{*}\partial G\bigl((\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2},(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\bigr)\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2}\\
\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\hat{\eta}_{1}-\eta_{1}^{*})

\displaystyle=\eta^{*}\sqrt{n}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{*})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}\theta_{2}^{*}}{(\sigma^{*})^{2/\alpha}}\biggr)^{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}\eta_{1}^{*}\partial G\bigl((\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2},(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\bigr)\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2}\\
\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(1).

Furthermore, we obtain from

\lambda_{1,1}+\theta_{0}=((\kappa^{2}+\eta^{2})/4+2\pi^{2})\theta_{2}
that

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\hat{\theta}_{0}-\theta_{0}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\Biggl[-\hat{\lambda}_{1,1}+\biggl(\frac{\hat{\kappa}^{2}+\hat{\eta}^{2}}{4}+2\pi^{2}\biggr)\hat{\theta}_{2}-\biggl\{-\lambda_{1,1}^{*}+\biggl(\frac{(\kappa^{*})^{2}+(\eta^{*})^{2}}{4}+2\pi^{2}\biggr)\theta_{2}^{*}\biggr\}\Biggr]

\displaystyle=-\sqrt{n}(\hat{\lambda}_{1,1}-\lambda_{1,1}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{n}\Biggl\{\biggl(\frac{\hat{\kappa}^{2}+\hat{\eta}^{2}}{4}-\frac{(\kappa^{*})^{2}+(\eta^{*})^{2}}{4}\biggr)\hat{\theta}_{2}+\biggl(\frac{(\kappa^{*})^{2}+(\eta^{*})^{2}}{4}+2\pi^{2}\biggr)(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{*})\Biggr\}

\displaystyle=-\sqrt{n}(\hat{\lambda}_{1,1}-\lambda_{1,1}^{*})+\biggl(\frac{(\kappa^{*})^{2}+(\eta^{*})^{2}}{4}+2\pi^{2}\biggr)\sqrt{n}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\sqrt{n}}{m^{1/2}N^{\gamma}}m^{1/2}N^{\gamma}\biggl(\frac{\hat{\kappa}^{2}+\hat{\eta}^{2}}{4}-\frac{(\kappa^{*})^{2}+(\eta^{*})^{2}}{4}\biggr)\hat{\theta}_{2}

\displaystyle=-\biggl(\frac{3\pi^{2}\theta_{2}^{*}}{(\sigma^{*})^{2}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}\partial H\bigl((\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2},(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\bigr)\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\sigma}_{1,1}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,1}^{*})^{2}\\
\hat{\sigma}_{1,2}^{2}-(\sigma_{1,2}^{*})^{2}\end{pmatrix}
